====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If both insureds die in the same accident and it cannot be determined who died first, when is the death benefit paid?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The death benefit will be paid immediately.
EVIDENCE: 9.1 If both insureds die in the same accident or incident and it cannot be determined who died first, the death benefit will be paid immediately.
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What is the rule for paying death benefits when two insured individuals die in the same accident and the order of death cannot be determined?
reasoning: This is a legal/insurance policy question involving a specific scenario: two insured individuals die in the same accident, and the order of death cannot be determined. The core issue is determining when the death benefit is paid under such circumstances. This depends on legal principles such as the "common disaster clause" or "simultaneous death clause" in life insurance policies, which typically state that if two people die in a common event and the order of death is undetermined, the benefit is paid as if the insureds died simultaneously. The key is to identify the standard rule in insurance law for such cases, which is usually that the benefit is paid upon the occurrence of the accident, not upon a specific death time. The query does not require identifying specific people, places, or documents, but rather a legal principle or policy rule. Therefore, this is a single-fact query that can be answered with a well-known insurance law principle.
  [0] the rule for paying death benefits when two insured individuals die in the same accident and the order of death is undetermined  (key_terms=['common disaster clause', 'simultaneous death clause', 'death benefit'], depends_on_previous=False)
planner LLM calls: 1  (total_tokens=0)

Logical Conclusions:
In cases of simultaneous death due to the same accident, the death benefit is paid immediately, a rule consistent across joint-life policies.
When two insureds die in the same incident and the order of death cannot be determined, the policy treats their deaths as simultaneous if they occur within 30 days of each other.
